### 1. 1011.6402v3.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/1011.6402v3.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 741640 bytes

- **Crux:** **Crux:** Order flow imbalance (OFI) at best bid/ask linearly predicts price change ΔP = β*OFI, β=1/(2*Depth), R² 65% vs volume 32%. OFI more robust than volume. Scaling gives sqrt(price vs volume). **Key:** Depth inverse slope explains intraday seasonality. **Relevance:** FVG = OFI proxy, validates adaptive SL max(3.5*spread,1.5*ATR).

- **Crux:** **Crux:** Parquet summary+footprint schema (Ask/Bid USD imbalance, POC ratio, liquidation Z-score), inner join on timestamp avoids duplication, JSON micro-updates, timestamp drift correction. Friction 41 bps (notional*0.00205*2), 7-stage ratchet Phase0 0.35R@0.8R → Phase5 3.3R@3.5R TP 3.0R time decay 24 bars <0.20R exit. Backtest +5.46% IS +4.75% OOS DD<25% but needs ML+footprint+LLM for 100%+. **Action:** Increase your Phase0 lock 0.15R→0.35R to cover friction.

### 9. Crypto Dynamic Hedging Strategy.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/Crypto Dynamic Hedging Strategy.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 338518 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/Crypto Dynamic Hedging Strategy.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Dynamic hedging multi-asset crypto (BTC ETH XRP) under prop firm strict drawdown 5% daily ($250 on $5k) 10% max ($4500 floor). Baseline: CVD EMA pullbacks, Volume Profile breakouts, VWAP 2-day reversals, ADX regime filter 15-50 trend, <35 mean-reversion. Position sizing 0.8-1.2% per trade 3-4 concurrent = 3.2-4.8% aggregate → single event exhausts daily risk. Vulnerability: correlation spikes >0.80-1.00 during liquidation cascades. Cascade phases: Catalyst, First-order liquidation (50-100x), Feedback loop ignition (20x tier), Systemic cascade (order book depletion, basis backwardation, breach 3.5% halt), Exhaustion (institutional absorption V-recovery). Triggers: OI spike without price, funding extremes, CVD divergence, footprint imbalance, liquidation velocity. **Action:** Engine needs hedging overlay + correlation filter.

- **Crux:** **Crux:** Cont et al 2021 JP Morgan Oxford — heterogeneous client order flow vs homogeneous Poisson/Hawkes. Introduces granular notation accounting for origin. Broker view: parent/meta orders sliced to child limit/market via Algos (VWAP, POV, SOR) across venues CBOE LSE XETRA. Data: large broker client flow. Segmentation into 4 stable clusters over time & stocks: quant, VWAP, signal, residual. Heterogeneity induces different order flow patterns. Suggests simple model per agent type. **Relevance:** Toxicity not uniform — need client-type-aware VPIN, VWAP flows less toxic than signal flows.

- **Crux:** **Crux:** Yildiz et al 2016 — 26 HFT firms 74% NASDAQ trades, upper bound profit $21B. VPIN volume-synchronized PIN metric, trade time clock (Clark 1973, Ane & Geman 2000). Negative relation HFT vs toxicity: HFTs reduce toxicity normally but drop out during high VPIN. VPIN predicts HFT liquidity supply/demand changes. HFT impact on variance not uniform — changes with toxicity/volume. Flash Crash May 6 2010 liquidity event where providers dropped. **Relevance:** Spread/ATR quarantine = VPIN proxy, spread widens 3x before toxic flow, volume-clock VPIN |buy-sell|/total over 50 trades quarantine if >0.6.

### 12. D1_Anticipating_2024_Kyle_Meets_Friedman_Informed_Trading_When_ssrn-4737312.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/D1_Anticipating_2024_Kyle_Meets_Friedman_Informed_Trading_When_ssrn-4737312.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 420276 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D1_Anticipating_2024_Kyle_Meets_Friedman_Informed_Trading_When_ssrn-4737312.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Yan et al 2024 Kyle meets Friedman: dynamic model monopolistic informed investor receives private info ongoing + post-trading disclosure (13f filings, HFT detection). Equilibrium reduces to info allocation = consumption-saving with borrowing constraint (Friedman permanent income). Investor decides how much info to utilize now vs dissimulate for future. Zero commitment value → two-player → single-player. Asymmetry: can dissimulate current for future but cannot borrow future info → borrowing constraint. **Relevance:** Kill zones 07-10/12-15 UTC when future info (CPI/NFP) anticipated, informed dissimulate → Judas sweeps, need wider SL 1.5*ATR floor.

### 13. D1_Canadian_2023_A_Snapshot-Conditioned_CVAE_Limit_Order_Book__ssrn-7438262.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/D1_Canadian_2023_A_Snapshot-Conditioned_CVAE_Limit_Order_Book__ssrn-7438262.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 1835661 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D1_Canadian_2023_A_Snapshot-Conditioned_CVAE_Limit_Order_Book__ssrn-7438262.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Snapshot-conditioned CVAE+LSTM LOB simulator for TSX Canadian equities (BMO, Telus). Representation: signed normalized depth vector at top k=3 bid/ask x_t=(B_k..B1,A1..Ak)∈R^{2k}, learns transition x_t→x_{t+Δ} homogenizing target vs heterogeneous order-by-order (type, price, size, delay). LSTM summarizes h=10 recent snapshots, β-weighted ELBO upweights rare price-change events, ablation h∈{1,2,4,10,20} confirms temporal context importance. Reproduces stylized facts: marginal queue distributions, LOB smile avg qty, level-to-level correlations, realistic price paths, reactive impact concave size-impact. Benchmark vs Conditional WGAN-GP: CVAE better queue error BMO 34.1 vs 55.3 Telus 52.8 vs 162.5 and correlation fidelity 0.34, WGAN lower KL 0.054 vs 0.143. **Relevance:** Reactive simulator for testing execution/market-making without production risk.

### 14. D1_Carol_2022_Price_Discovery_in_Bitcoin_The_Role_of_Limit__ssrn-4150979.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/D1_Carol_2022_Price_Discovery_in_Bitcoin_The_Role_of_Limit__ssrn-4150979.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 1863258 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D1_Carol_2022_Price_Discovery_in_Bitcoin_The_Role_of_Limit__ssrn-4150979.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Price discovery Bitcoin-Dollar on Coinbase Feb-May 2022 120 days 24/7 tick-by-tick. Modified RL framework vs VAR — captures nonlinear concave price impact vs size, linear time vs exponential. Findings: market orders individually 2-3x more informative than limit, but limit orders abundance = 50% price discovery. Asymmetry: sell market orders carry more info than buy (unidirectional retail flows). Negligible tick size → undercutting best quotes main contribution. Order book imbalance increases contribution along imbalance, binding spread increases market order importance. Information flows from other exchanges (Binance) influence Coinbase. **Relevance:** Limit order flow critical for FVG, retail asymmetry explains Judas sweeps.

Predicts future volatility (std dev), liquidity: inverse relation adverse selection ↑ → realized spread ↑, order-book slope ↑, Amihud illiquidity ↑, but no significant impact on depth. Same impact bid/ask side. **Relevance:** 10% spread is adverse selection → your 3.5*spread floor covers it, toxicity proxy.

### 43. D1_Working_2025_Verma_Research_Capital_VRC_ssrn-6301779.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/D1_Working_2025_Verma_Research_Capital_VRC_ssrn-6301779.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 419402 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D1_Working_2025_Verma_Research_Capital_VRC_ssrn-6301779.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Verma Research Capital 2025 Detecting Liquidity Stress Before Crises: Order Flow Toxicity, VPIN, Hidden Fragility. Proposition: liquidity not state but emergent outcome incentives, balance sheet constraints, confidence. Deterioration sequence: microstructure → derivatives → price dislocation. Examples: Flash Crash May6 2010 depth deteriorating hours before 2:45PM, March 2020 Treasury depth late Feb spreads widening, Aug 2015 overnight futures imbalances. Theoretical foundations: adverse selection spread model, Kyle lambda price impact, Amihud illiquidity, PIN/VPIN volume imbalances. Propagation: sequential deterioration, funding liquidity + market liquidity spiral, cross-asset contagion common ownership. Systematic liquidity providers: conditional algorithmic market making, synchronized withdrawal → liquidity void, gamma hedging amplification. VRC Liquidity Stress Indicator (LSI) composite daily: Kyle Lambda trend 20%, VPIN level/trend 25%, Bid-Ask spread divergence 15%, Amihud trend 15%, Options skew/depth 25%. Regime classification. Derivatives signals: put skew informed flow, term structure kinks near-term risk concentration, cost of rolling liquidity premium. Execution quality: implementation shortfall, spread capture ratio health metric. **Relevance:** Your spread/ATR quarantine + VPIN proxy + LSI-like composite needed.

- **Crux:** **Crux:** BV-VPIN Bulk Volume VPIN measuring order flow toxicity + liquidity international equities daily data. Toxicity = exposure to private info counter-parties, high toxicity → market makers lose, liquidity drops, price falls. Flash Crash May6 2010 998.5 DJIA points extreme lack liquidity, Easley et al VPIN CDF predicted >1 hour advance. BV-VPIN version uses bulk volume classification, closely linked to Hi-Lo spreads permanent price effect, incrementally better than original VPIN. Applied to international indices daily, rise in BV-VPIN foreshadows high volatility. If BV-VPIN futures existed → safe haven characteristics during downturns. Active strategy timing equities vs risk-free when BV-VPIN low/high outperforms buy-and-hold. **Relevance:** BV-VPIN works on daily not just HFT, supports your daily LSI-like indicator, volume bucket size VBS adjustable for transitory vs permanent info.

- **Crux:** **Crux:** Funding rates conditional informativeness Binance XAUUSDT gold perpetual (commodity-linked, launched Jan 2026) 203,037 1-min obs. Order flow proxies no unconditional predictive ability: trade-based OFI insignificant in-sample, negative OOS R2, fails multiple-testing. Active channel is funding: funding rate positively predicts returns 5/15/30min t=2.22,2.95,3.71, funding-by-orderflow interaction negative significant t=-2.64,-2.28,-2.26. Positive funding = persistence + leveraged-driven demand, negative interaction = crowded reversal (additional buying when crowded → reversal). Session patterns European window stable sign but fragile not survive corrections. Effects small not profitable net costs. **Relevance:** In benchmark-anchored perpetuals (gold, BTC) funding > flow, positioning-and-reversal channel, not classical informed trading.

- **Path:** `papers/D4_Beyond_2025_Deep_Learning_for_VWAP_Execution_in_Crypto_Ma_ssrn-5150912 (1).pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 17651632 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D4_Beyond_2025_Deep_Learning_for_VWAP_Execution_in_Crypto_Ma_ssrn-5150912 (1).pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Genet 2025 Deep Learning for VWAP Execution in Crypto Beyond Volume Curve: VWAP benchmark unbiased standard. Traditional two-step: forecast volume curve then allocate, assumption holds steady but suboptimal volatile crypto prediction error higher. Proposes direct optimization bypassing intermediate volume curve via automatic differentiation custom loss minimizing VWAP slippage. Results: direct optimization achieves lower slippage than conventional even with naive linear model, strategies optimized for VWAP diverge from accurate volume curve predictions. **Relevance:** Directly optimize your execution objective (slippage) not just volume forecasting, use custom loss.

### 72. D4_Clustering_2023_ClusterLOB_Enhancing_Trading_Strategies_by_ssrn-5236285.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/D4_Clustering_2023_ClusterLOB_Enhancing_Trading_Strategies_by_ssrn-5236285.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 4396006 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D4_Clustering_2023_ClusterLOB_Enhancing_Trading_Strategies_by_ssrn-5236285.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** ClusterLOB method clustering individual market events in MBO (market-by-order) data. Each event augmented with 6 time-dependent features, K-means++ assigns to 3 clusters: directional (informed large impact), opportunistic (reacts short-term fluctuations), market-making (provides liquidity minimizing inventory). Data: 1 year MBO NASDAQ small/medium/large-tick stocks. Compute OFI across each cluster within 30-min buckets as alpha signal. Best clustering-driven strategy highest Sharpe in training outperforms benchmarks in test. Also evaluates OFI decomposition across add/cancel/trade events. **Relevance:** Decompose your FVG OFI by cluster type — directional OFI more predictive than opportunistic.

### 85. D4_Justin_2016_Deep_Learning_for_Limit_Order_Books_ssrn-2710331.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/D4_Justin_2016_Deep_Learning_for_Limit_Order_Books_ssrn-2710331.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 587393 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D4_Justin_2016_Deep_Learning_for_Limit_Order_Books_ssrn-2710331.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Sirignano 2016 spatial neural network for distributions on R^d, efficient architecture exploiting LOB structure, low-dimensional model price movements deep into book (many levels beyond best bid/ask). Models joint distribution of future LOB state conditional on current state. Outperforms naive empirical, logistic regression with nonlinear features, standard NN. Especially outperforms in tail distribution important for risk management. Trained on ~500 US stocks, 50 GPUs cluster, dropout, batch norm. Local spatial structure: price movements decomposed into sequence of local steps. Well-posed for R^d. **Relevance:** Deep LOB for FVG prediction, use deeper book levels not just top.

- **Path:** `papers/D5_Crypto_2024_Using_Machines_to_Advance_Better_Models_of_th_ssrn-4986862.pdf`
- **Folder:** root
- **Size:** 346280 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/D5_Crypto_2024_Using_Machines_to_Advance_Better_Models_of_th_ssrn-4986862.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Crypto return cross-section machine learning factor model. 33 portfolios 5,389 coins/tokens, 13 factors, Jan 2014-Jan 2024 525 weekly cross-sections. Proposes crypto-eight-factor model vs Liu Tsyvinski Wu 2022 three-factor (market, size, momentum). Three-stage process: minimize pricing errors + regularization to get beta coefficients, OOS R² via 5-fold cross-validation penalty ratio, select factors maximizing OOS R². SDF representation. Eight-factor outperforms three-factor in predicting average returns. **Relevance:** Factor selection methodology applicable to your 18-asset Forex/CFD portfolio, suggests 8 factors not just 3.

### 112. ssrn-1002437.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/Master_Batch_1/ssrn-1002437.pdf`
- **Folder:** Master_Batch_1
- **Size:** 358117 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/Master_Batch_1/ssrn-1002437.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Justice Between Authors (Law, non-trading) — Dawn Nunziato GWU 2002. Theory of intergenerational justice between authors building on Rawls fairness + just savings principle. Authors increasingly use private ordering encryption + clickwrap licenses to create unlimited rights in digital works (e-books, DVD) disregarding future authors, while benefiting from limitations on predecessors (idea/expression dichotomy, fair use, limited term). Behind veil of ignorance, authors would agree ex ante to substantial limitations (savings) preserving raw materials of creative process. Landes & Posner economic analysis supports. Epstein market objection insufficient for creative savings. **Relevance:** Low for trading engine, but illustrates IP regime for data licensing.

### 117. ssrn-1343726.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/Master_Batch_1/ssrn-1343726.pdf`
- **Folder:** Master_Batch_1
- **Size:** 1308567 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/Master_Batch_1/ssrn-1343726.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Duration Hidden Markov Model regimes bull/bear — Ntantamis 2009 CREATES Aarhus. Process occupies small number states/regimes, durations of states and factors affecting durations interest. Traditional bull/bear identification. Introduces Duration HMM where duration of each Markov Chain state is random variable depends on exogenous variables (short-term interest rate, interest rate spread). Model allows endogenous determination regimes + estimates effect explanatory variables on durations. Estimated on NYSE returns: bull regime higher mean lower variability, bull duration negatively dependent on short rates positively on spread, bear duration positively on short rate negatively on spread. Extends Hamilton 1989 time-homogeneous transition probabilities, Diebold et al 1994 Filardo 1994 time-varying. **Relevance:** Regime duration modeling for your ADX regime filter, interest rates affect bull/bear durations — use rates as feature.

### 655. ssrn-1273160.pdf
- **Path:** `papers/Mater_Batch_2/ssrn-1273160.pdf`
- **Folder:** Mater_Batch_2
- **Size:** 521087 bytes
- **Raw:** https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kbsingh1399/Trading/main/papers/Mater_Batch_2/ssrn-1273160.pdf
- **Crux:** **Crux:** Cont Stoikov Talreja 2008 Columbia stochastic model for order book dynamics — continuous-time Markov process tracking number limit orders at each price level, balance easily estimated from data, captures empirical properties, analytical tractability via Laplace transform. Computes conditional probabilities: midprice increase next move, execution at bid before ask moves, execution both buy/sell at best quotes before price moves, given state of book. Queueing analogy limit orders wait queue to be executed against market orders/canceled. Tokyo Stock Exchange data. Related to Parlour 1998 Foucault 2005 Rosu equilibrium models, Bouchaud Farmer empirical features. **Relevance:** Foundational LOB queueing model, your FVG imbalance similar to queue sizes, Laplace methods for fast probability computation.
